### 5.12 A resumed partial run moved its answers to the page's parts (found in review 2026-10-22, fixed the same day; a saved bike's remainder escalated — and most of it fixed in §5.13)

**Who measured.** **[RA]** and **[RB]**, two adversarial reviews of
`w5/partial-checkup` at `fb05614` (§5.10 and §5.11 above), quoted from their
findings: jsdom with the real page, wizard and actions on PostgreSQL, a
recording fake, probes over the seven presets, mutations, and — [RA] only —
Chromium 153 on a production build with the test hooks. **[I4]** the
implementer of the same evening, on the same branch: every finding
reproduced first, at `fb05614`, with the reviewers' own files copied into
this tree (`tests/integration/zz-*.test.ts` and their likes, removed again);
then the fix, its tests, and the measurements below.

**What was ruled before** (§5.10, §5.11): resume on the page's scope; refuse
an empty parts scope; never trim a full run; and a checkup limited to some
parts acts on parts inside them only. No schema change.

#### Symptom

The scope rule made ONE question key mean different parts under different
scopes — `check-brakes-disc#pad-wear` is about the rear pads in a
rear-caliper checkup and about the front ones in a front-caliper checkup —
and the resume rule of §5.10 held a run on any page that ASKED its questions.
So [I4, reproduced at `fb05614` with [RA]'s cases, on PostgreSQL]:

- **R1/B1, a saved bike**: `?parts=brake-caliper-rear`, the pads KO
  `pad-worn`, leave; `?parts=brake-caliper-front`: "Contrôle partiel", no
  notice, the summary said "Ne compte que pour : Plaquettes ou patins avant";
  finished, the run's item was `pad-wear=KO@brake-caliper-front` and the list
  `["REPLACE brake-pads-front"]` — the worn rear pads gone. The case was
  pinned in `tests/integration/checkup-resume.test.ts` "as a decision".
- **R2, a guest**, the same sequence: `va:buildlist:demo` =
  `["replace brake-pads-front"]`, the stored scope rewritten from the rear
  caliper to the front one. [RA] saw R1 and R2 in Chromium, 8 runs of 8.
- **R3**: both calipers, the pads KO, then the rear caliper alone: the list
  `["REPLACE brake-pads-rear"]`, the front pads' finding dropped, no word.
- **R4, a guest**: the rear pads OK, then both calipers: the rear-only OK
  shown as the OK of the both-sides question, no covered sentence.
- **R5/RA-2, a rim-brake bike**: the front wheel's rim track KO
  `rim-brake-track-worn`, then the front caliper, whose rim-track question
  offers only `rim-contaminated`: the next save left the row
  `{reasonKeys: [], partId: brake-caliper-front}`; finished, the list said
  `["CLEAN brake-pads-front"]`.
- **R6/RA-3/B2, the saved bike's resume banner**: a rear-only OK, then the
  banner (no `?parts=`): "Contrôle complet" and one generic sentence; the
  rest answered OK and finished: `REPLACE brake-pads-front recheck-ok`,
  `REPLACE brake-pads-rear recheck-ok`, both calipers `OK`. Through the
  actions ([RB]'s payload): the row went from `@brake-caliper-rear` to
  `@brake-caliper-front`.
- **B6, a guest's run of before the deploy** (the old planner asked
  `hose-leak` of a caliper): `[rear caliper]`, pads KO, alignment OK,
  `hose-leak` OK, finished — imported as `FULL`, its items on the FRONT
  caliper and lever, the front caliper `BROKEN`, the rear one nothing.
- **B4**: `writeColumn`'s owner predicate removed, every shipped test green
  ([RB], mutation M25). [I4]: [RB]'s case that exercises it passes at
  `fb05614` — the predicate is there, and nothing shipped exercised it.
- **B5**: the "a question the bike is no longer asked widens nothing" filter
  removed from the page, or from the actions, every shipped test green ([RB],
  M22–M24).
- **RA-5**: the left shifter alone now plans no question ([I4]'s probe:
  "shifter-left: now 0 (old 1)") — §5.11 did not say so.
- **Words** (RA-4, B3, B7, B8, B9): CLAUDE.md said "or under another part
  sharing a question — counts its answers for that question's other parts
  too, which the notice says" (no notice was shown, and the answer moved);
  `scope.ts` said "the run wins over the page — it is never trimmed"; the
  actions' header "never a reason to delete" (R5 deleted a symptom);
  `docs/backlog.md` gave "a saved bike's row cannot" as the reason to defer;
  CHANGELOG said 1 685 interface strings per language ([I4] counted 1 686 at
  `0ad0982` and 1 691 at `fb05614`); CLAUDE.md quoted the step's sentence as
  the summary's; §5.10 said "only a narrower page" leaves a stored answer out
  (a tab holding an older copy of the run does too).

#### Root cause

`effectiveScope` and `resumeScope` compared step KEYS only, while since
§5.11 what a key's answer is about depends on the scope. And `writeItems`
rewrote every row of the accepted state, so a stored row the payload did not
carry was re-stamped with the part and the symptoms of whatever plan the
payload had been accepted against.

#### Fix

The fix as it stood at `5c15de8`. Four of its bullets were changed by the
second round of review the same evening (§5.13), and say so where they stand.

- **One rule, `holds`** (`lib/checkup/scope.ts`): a scope holds a run when it
  asks every answer about what it was given for — it asks the question; it
  still offers the symptom a KO was given with (`scopeFacts().withheld`); and,
  for a saved bike's row, the part the row is recorded against is inside it
  (§5.13: "inside" was not enough — the page must record it against that
  same part).
  The actions (`effectiveScope`), the import, the page (a saved bike) and the
  wizard (a guest) all decide by it. An answer to a question the corpus
  dropped still decides nothing.
- **Where a run that is not held goes** (`resumeTarget`): a GUEST's run knows
  its parts, and goes back to them (`?parts=…&resume=own`, "Un contrôle
  limité à : … est déjà en cours sur ce vélo…") — from other parts AND from
  the full page — and on from there to the full checkup only when none of
  its answers was given for some of the parts its question names
  (`coveredParts`): then the full checkup asks each about exactly what it was
  given for. A SAVED bike's run does not know its parts and carries on as the
  full checkup, which loses nothing. A full run is never narrowed, as before.
  (§5.13: `resumeTarget` became `guestResumeTarget`, which takes `?resume=`
  at its word nowhere, and `savedResumeTarget`, which reads a saved bike's
  parts from its address and its rows and carries on as the full checkup
  only when neither tells.)
- **The notice names the questions** whose answers now count for every part
  they name (`resumed.partialAsFullNamed`, FR and EN): RA-3's "at the least".
- **A row the payload does not carry is never rewritten**: `writeItems`
  writes the payload's questions and no others (R5's stripped symptom
  through another tab). (§5.13: this did NOT fix B2's moved row, as this
  bullet first said — the banner page carries every restored answer, so
  its payload carries the row; what fixed it is that such a run is no longer
  carried on as the full checkup.)
- **Questions the scope only TOUCHES** (`scopeFacts().touched`: the step's
  hosts meet a picked part's; the old planner asked it, today's does not): an
  OK or a skip there widens nothing and is left out of the run's state — the
  third reason an answer is dropped, beside a withdrawn verdict and a
  question the corpus dropped; for a saved bike its row stays as it is. A KO
  there widens, and its finding is kept. The import reports a partial
  checkup whose only answers are such `nothing-kept`. (§5.13, V1-3: no ruling
  allowed that third drop. Such an answer is now KEPT — in a guest's
  storage, as an imported row — and still acted on nowhere.)
- **Fixtures that can be what browsers hold**: `tests/_helpers/guest-work.ts`
  plans with the old rule (`planBeforeTheScopeRule`) for `resumeGuestRun` by
  default and for `guestRun` on request. `lib/guest/import.test.ts`'s
  "continued on ANOTHER" case is back to the three answers an old browser
  holds (it had been cut to two because the helper planned with today's
  planner).
- **Words**: every sentence above corrected where it stood; CHANGELOG now
  says 1 694 (three messages added here).

#### What it does not do, and why: a column (escalated)

(As escalated at `5c15de8`. §5.13 found that the rows tell more than the
table below credits them with — a row on the rear caliper excludes the front
one — and that the page's own address can tell the rest of a reload; what
still needs a column is in §5.13, "What it does not do".)

A saved bike's `PARTIAL` row records no parts (`Checkup.scope` is
`FULL | PARTIAL`), and its items cannot stand in for them. Each names ONE
part, `partIds[0]` of the step it was written under:

| The run's picked parts | the pads' item names  | the parts that answer was for |
| ---------------------- | --------------------- | ----------------------------- |
| the rear caliper       | `brake-caliper-rear`  | the rear pads                 |
| the front caliper      | `brake-caliper-front` | the front pads                |
| both calipers          | `brake-caliper-front` | both pads                     |
| either, before the fix | `brake-caliper-front` | whatever was picked (§5.11)   |

"Front" and "both" read the same, and an item written before the deploy says
nothing at all. What follows:

- carried on as the full checkup (the banner, or parts that do not hold the
  run), an answer given for one side counts for every part its question
  names — measured: R1's finished list holds both sides' pads, R6's closes
  the front pads' line — said on screen, the questions named;
- a run on the front caliper reopened on both calipers, or the reverse, is
  held, and its answer then counts for the page's parts. Measured [I4], with
  a throwaway probe on the harness of `checkup-resume.test.ts`, on
  PostgreSQL: the front pads' OK, then both calipers — "Contrôle partiel", no
  notice, the questions asked whole; finished, BOTH calipers `OK`. Both
  calipers' pads KO, then the front caliper — no notice, the summary's "Ne
  compte que pour : Plaquettes ou patins avant"; finished, the list
  `["REPLACE brake-pads-front"]`, the rear pads' finding not on it;
- a rear-caliper run of before the deploy (its pads' item on the front
  caliper), reloaded on its own `?parts=`: measured by the same probe —
  "Contrôle complet" and the notice naming « Mesurer l’usure des plaquettes ».

No other column of `Checkup` or `CheckupItem` can say it without changing
what that column means. `docs/backlog.md` has the column it would take; it
needs a ruling.

#### What production rows, and browsers, may look like (nothing is rewritten)

No data migration. Saved bikes: unfinished `PARTIAL` runs of before the
deploy hold items on their question's first part (§5.11), and are judged by
it (above). A row a payload does not carry now stays as it was written — a
pre-deploy `hose-leak` OK in a caliper's run among them, which a `PARTIAL`
checkup then finishes with, unacted on. Browsers: guest runs answered under
the old planner (§5.11, "Browsers hold such checkups too").

### 5.13 A saved bike's partial run still counted its answers for parts they were not given for; a guest's trusted a stale address (found in the second review 2026-10-22, fixed the same day; one remainder needs a column)

**Who measured.** **[V1]** the second adversarial review of
`w5/partial-checkup` at `5c15de8` (§5.12 above), quoted from its findings:
jsdom with the real page, wizard and actions on PostgreSQL, Chromium 153 on
a production build with the test hooks, and mutations. **[I5]** the
implementer of the same evening, on the same branch: every finding
reproduced first, at `5c15de8`, with [V1]'s integration file copied into this
tree (`tests/integration/zz-verify-1.test.ts`, removed again); then the fix,
its tests and the measurements below. All of [I5]'s figures are jsdom and
PostgreSQL unless a line says Chromium.

**What was ruled before** (§5.10, §5.11): resume on the page's scope; refuse
an empty parts scope; never trim a full run; a checkup limited to some parts
acts on parts inside them only; no schema change. Not part of this unit, by
ruling: a KO stored as `ATTENTION`, a hosted part's badge, the yes/no
refinement control.

#### Symptom [I5, reproduced at `5c15de8`]

A saved bike (the gravel preset; "rear" is the rear caliper):

- **RA-1/B1, VS-R1**: the rear pads KO, then `?parts=brake-caliper-front`:
  "Contrôle complet" and the notice naming « Mesurer l’usure des
  plaquettes »; the first save on that page moved the row from
  `pad-wear=KO@brake-caliper-rear` to `@brake-caliper-front`; finished,
  `REPLACE brake-pads-front` AND `REPLACE brake-pads-rear`, both calipers
  `ATTENTION` — the front never looked at.
- **RA-1, VS-BF**: both calipers, the pads KO, then the front caliper:
  "Contrôle partiel", no notice; finished, `["REPLACE brake-pads-front"]`,
  `{brake-caliper-front: ATTENTION}` — the rear pads' finding dropped.
- **RA-1, VS-FB**: an earlier full checkup's two open pads lines; the front
  pads OK, then both calipers: "Contrôle partiel", no notice; finished, both
  lines `recheck-ok` and the REAR caliper `OK`.
- **RA-1, VS-PRE**: a rear-caliper run written as `main` writes it (its pads'
  row on the FRONT caliper), opened on the front caliper: held, no notice;
  finished, `["REPLACE brake-pads-front"]`.
- **RA-3/B2, VS-R6, VS-B2**: the rear pads OK (or KO), then the resume
  banner: "Contrôle complet", 33 of 34 questions shown, the pads' skipped as
  answered; one save moved the row to the front caliper; finished, both
  pads' lines `recheck-ok` and both calipers `OK`. [RB]'s payload of §5.12
  replayed on the actions (VS-SBANNER) gave the same rows.
- **VS-R5**, a rim-brake bike: the front wheel's rim track worn, then the
  front caliper: "Contrôle complet", the notice naming « Contrôler les flancs
  de jante »; finished, both wheels `INSPECT_SHOP`.

A guest (the demo bike):

- **V1-1, VG-OWN**: a FRONT-caliper run, pads KO, then the address
  `?parts=brake-caliper-rear&resume=own`: no redirect, "Un contrôle limité
  à : Étrier de frein arrière est déjà en cours…", the pads' row "ne
  comptera que pour : Plaquettes ou patins arrière"; finished,
  `["replace brake-pads-rear"]`, the stored scope rewritten to the rear
  caliper. [V1] saw it in Chromium too, 4 of 4, and reached that address by
  Back twice (V1-1b).
- **V1-2, VG-FULL**: an earlier full checkup's two lines; a rear-caliper run,
  pads OK, then `?resume=full`: no redirect, "Contrôle complet"; finished,
  both lines `recheck-ok`. [V1] in Chromium, 4 of 4.
- **V1-3, VG-TOUCHED**: a rear-caliper run of before the deploy holding
  `hose-leak: ok`: after one answer on its own page, storage held the pads
  and the new answer, and no `hose-leak`.

The import (`importGuestState` on PostgreSQL):

- **B6, VI-B6KO**: an old rear-caliper checkup, pads KO, alignment OK,
  `hose-leak` KO: `FULL`, items on the front caliper and lever, states
  `{brake-caliper-front: BROKEN, brake-lever-front: BROKEN}`, nothing on the
  rear.
- **B6, VI-RIM-REAR**: an old rear-caliper rim-brake checkup, rim track worn:
  `FULL`, `rim-track=KO@wheel-front`, `{wheel-front: BROKEN}`.
- **V1-3, VI-B6OK**: the same with `hose-leak` OK: `PARTIAL`, the `hose-leak`
  answer left out, reported `imported` (the browser then forgets it).

And two findings about words: **V1-4** — §6's check for §5.12 called the
saved bike's shipped list a defect, while accepting a list with both pads;
**RA-5** — the left shifter alone plans no question (unchanged, below).

#### Root cause [read]

- `holds` asked of a saved bike's row only that its part be INSIDE the page's
  scope. A row on the rear caliper is inside "both calipers", whose question
  is about both sides: held, the rear-only answer counted for both.
- A saved bike's run that a page did not hold always went to the full
  checkup, though its row could say which side it was for: each row names
  the first part its question reported on (`answeredItems`, `partIds[0]` of
  the NARROWED step, `lib/checkup/plan.ts`), hosts in the guide's order — so
  a row on the rear caliper, the last of the pads' question's two, excludes
  the front one.
- The row on the front caliper is ambiguous — front alone, or both, or (a
  row written before 2026-10-22, which named the first host of the WHOLE
  question) anything — and `holds` read it as "the front caliper": a
  both-calipers run, or a rear one of before the deploy, was held on the
  front caliper.
- `resumeTarget` took `?resume=own` and `?resume=full` at their word: an
  arrival flag that says why the wizard sent the visitor, not which run it
  sent, and history keeps it for later runs.
- The wizard's `restore` married a guest's run to the page's plan
  (`reconcile`) and wrote it back whole: a touched question's answer, not in
  the plan, was dropped from storage at the next save; the import's
  `effectiveScope` left it out the same way, and widened a checkup to `FULL`
  for a touched KO or a withheld symptom — and a `FULL` import writes each
  state on the item's first part, the front one.
- After the banner the wizard restored and posted EVERY stored answer under
  `scope: full`, so §5.12's "a row the payload does not carry is never
  rewritten" did not reach B2: the payload carried the row.

#### Fix

- **`holds` compares the part** (`lib/checkup/scope.ts`): a stored row is
  held only by a page that would record it against the SAME part — its
  question's first part there. Neither the front caliper's page nor both
  calipers' holds a rear caliper's row.
- **A saved bike's parts, from what can tell them** (`savedResumeTarget`,
  decided in `page.tsx`):
  - `?run=` — the run's `startedAt` in ms. The wizard writes a saved bike's
    address as what is on screen (`parts` the scope shown, `run` beside it on
    a partial page, never `resume`); a page whose `?run=` is the unfinished
    run's and which gives every row back its part is the run's own: a
    reload, Back and Forward keep it there.
  - the rows (`recordedScope`): a row says its part was picked and every
    host its question names BEFORE it was not; the parts are told when every
    host each answered question names AFTER its row is named or excluded by
    some row, and the named hosts, planned, give each row back its part. Told,
    the run resumes on the page when the page asks every answer as those parts
    did (`sameEffects`), otherwise on those parts — planned by the page itself,
    `resumedOwn`, the own-parts sentence, the address rewritten — or on the
    full checkup when that checkup takes it over (`carriedOnWhole`: no answer
    narrowed there, or a touched KO / withheld symptom). Not told, the full
    checkup.
  - `tests/unit/checkup/recorded-scope.test.ts` replays every one-part pick
    and every pair of hosts a question names, on the seven presets, for every
    prefix of the plan answered: the rows tell the parts of 58 to 69 of each
    preset's 115 to 140 runs, and every time the parts they tell ask each
    answered question with the same parts and findings as the run did. Rows
    written by the planner of before 2026-10-22 are never read as narrowing an
    answer given to a whole question.
- **A partial run carried on as the full checkup is shown again**: a saved
  bike's wizard opens no later than the first answer the notice names
  (`restore`; before, on the first open question, after it). A guest's never
  needs it: the full checkup takes a guest's run over only when it asks
  every answer about what it was given for. (§5.14: not so of a guest's run
  whose own parts ask nothing on the bike — the code of this section sent it
  round the empty page; the full checkup now takes it over, and opens no
  later than its first named answer too. Nor, found by the fourth
  verification, V3-2, of one whose own parts no longer ask ONE of its
  answers since the guest bike was replaced: the full checkup took it over
  and counted a front-only answer for both sides; the cursor rule now
  covers it, below.)
- **A guest's `?resume=` decides no part** (`guestResumeTarget`): a run on
  other parts than the page's goes back to its own whatever the address
  says (V1-1); the full checkup is reached with the run's own parts
  (`?parts=<own>&resume=full`), which the page plans and hands the wizard
  (`widenedFrom`), and the wizard stays only if those are the stored run's
  parts and the full checkup takes it over (V1-2). A run is sent back only
  by the parts the page will read back the same way (`knownParts`).
- **Touched answers are kept, never acted on** (V1-3): the wizard puts them
  back into the restored state (`keepTouched`) — every reader goes through
  `state.steps`, so nothing shows, lists or tints them — and every save
  writes them; the import writes them as rows.
- **The import keeps an old partial checkup PARTIAL** (B6): an answer to a
  question the scope only touches, or a KO whose symptom the narrowed
  question withholds, is a row (against `touchedPart` — the first host of
  the whole question the scope picked — or the narrowed question's first
  part), with its verdict, note and symptoms, and no part state and no link
  (`acted: false`). Only an answer the scope neither asks nor touches widens
  it to `FULL` (the old wizard's stale scope).
- **Words**: the three statements that called B2 fixed (`actions.ts`,
  §5.12's Fix, CLAUDE.md), §6's §5.12 check (V1-4), §5.10's table,
  `docs/backlog.md`, the import's and the page's headers, `load.ts`,
  `types.ts` (a state's `answers` may now hold the kept ones) and
  `tests/_helpers/guest-work.ts`.
- **Not changed**: VS-SBANNER's payload — `scope: full` carrying the rear KO
  — is a full checkup's, and the actions act on it as one, both sides: they
  cannot tell it from a visitor re-answering the pads on the full page. The
  real banner no longer posts it for such a run (B2's test, below). RA-5:
  the left shifter alone still plans no question; the backlog entry asks the
  maintainer.

#### What it does not do, and why: a column (still escalated)

- A run whose rows cannot tell — a front-side run, a both-sides one, a row
  written before the deploy — opened anywhere but on its own address (the
  parts panel picking the same parts again, the resume banner, another
  device) carries on as the full checkup: its answers count for every part
  their question names. Said, named and shown first; one verdict per
  question cannot be one per side (VS-FB finished there: both pads' lines
  `recheck-ok`, measured). Before this fix such a run picked again from the
  panel was resumed partial; it now goes to the full checkup — the price of
  not holding the both-sides run on one side.
- `?run=` is the browser's word, taken only where the page gives every row
  back its part: a hand-edited address could hold a both-calipers run on
  its front side.
- VS-R5's front wheel is the first of the rim-track question's four hosts:
  that run carries on as the full checkup, and a confirmed KO lists both
  wheels.

`docs/backlog.md` ("A saved bike's partial checkup does not remember its
parts") has the column it would take.

#### What production rows, and browsers, may look like (nothing is rewritten)

No data migration. A saved bike's unfinished `PARTIAL` run written before
the deploy has every row on its question's first part: wherever it is opened
it carries on as the full checkup (said, the answers shown first), and its
rows then name their whole question's first part, as a full checkup's do. An
unfinished one written by this branch's earlier builds was never deployed.
Guest browsers hold runs of the old planner: their touched answers are now
kept, and imported as rows nothing acts on. Imported checkups of before this
fix were written by `main`'s import, which knew no scope rule (§5.11).

### 5.14 A guest's run on parts that ask nothing went round the empty page for ever; three sentences said more than the code did (found in the third verification 2026-10-23, fixed the same day)

**Who measured.** **[V2]** the third adversarial verification of
`w5/partial-checkup`, at the pre-rebase tip `76c276d` (the same code as the
rebased `5909664`), quoted from its findings: jsdom with the real page,
wizard and actions on PostgreSQL, and Chromium on a production build with the
test hooks. **[I6]** the implementer of 2026-10-23, on the rebased branch:
[V2]'s integration file copied into this tree under another name (removed
again) and run at `5909664` before any change; then the fix, its tests and
the measurements below. All of [I6]'s figures are jsdom and PostgreSQL unless
a line says Chromium.

#### Symptom [I6, reproduced at `5909664`; Chromium: V2]

- **V2-1, VG-TRAP2** (today's planner): a guest bike `road-rim-2x11`,
  `?parts=front-derailleur`, the shifting question answered OK; then the
  guest bike is a `gravel-1x11`, as the tree writes it (nothing clears
  `va:checkup:local`). `?spec=<1x>`, `?…&parts=chain` and
  `?…&parts=brake-caliper-front`: each "Reprise du contrôle en cours…" and a
  full navigation to `?spec=…&parts=front-derailleur&resume=own`, which the
  page rendered as the empty page — no wizard. That page's one link, "Lancer
  un contrôle complet", is `?spec=…`, the first address again. [V2] saw it
  in Chromium on desktop and mobile, in French and English, and the bike
  page's "Reprendre le contrôle" leads to `?spec=…&parts=front-derailleur`,
  the empty page too. No checkup could be done on that bike until storage was
  cleared.
- **V2-1, VG-TRAP** (the planner of before 2026-10-22): a guest's left-shifter
  run on `road-rim-2x11`, the shifting question answered OK — asked then,
  asked nothing today (RA-5). The same three addresses, the same redirect,
  to `?…&parts=shifter-left&resume=own`, the same empty page.
- **V2-2**, words: CLAUDE.md's bold rule said a run's "answers are never
  moved to parts they were not given for, and the server never deletes or
  rewrites an answer for a page's sake"; `docs/backlog.md`, that no stored
  answer is "rewritten for a page's sake". At `5909664`: VS-SAME — a
  front-caliper run, the pads KO, the front caliper picked again without
  `?run=` — "Contrôle complet" and the notice naming « Mesurer l’usure des
  plaquettes »; finished, `REPLACE brake-pads-front` AND
  `REPLACE brake-pads-rear`, both calipers `ATTENTION`. VS-SBANNER — a save
  under the rear caliper, then a finish under the full scope — the row
  `pad-wear=KO@brake-caliper-rear` became `@brake-caliper-front`. VS-R5 — a
  front wheel's rim track worn, then the front caliper — both wheels
  `INSPECT_SHOP`. The last paragraph of the same CLAUDE.md bullet described
  exactly that ("such a run carries on as the full checkup, its answers
  counting for every part their question names").
- **V2-3, VI-B6KO-UNFINISHED**: an old rear-caliper run, the pads KO and
  `hose-leak` KO, NOT finished, imported: `PARTIAL`, `IN_PROGRESS`, both rows
  on the rear caliper, no line, no state. The account's banner page:
  "Contrôle complet", the notice naming « Mesurer l’usure des plaquettes » et
  « Chercher une fuite de liquide », the pads' question on screen. Finished:
  `FULL`, the rows on the front caliper and lever, the lines
  `INSPECT_SHOP brake-line-front`, `INSPECT_SHOP brake-line-rear`,
  `REPLACE brake-pads-front`, `REPLACE brake-pads-rear`, both calipers and
  both levers `ATTENTION`. CLAUDE.md's import paragraph said such a run
  resumes "on the parts its rows tell (a rear-caliper run's, the touched rows
  included, name the rear caliper)", and `lib/guest/import.ts` that such a KO
  is "NEVER ACTED ON" — both true of a COMPLETED import only.
- **RA-5**: unchanged — the left shifter alone still plans no question
  (`tests/unit/checkup/plan-corpus.test.ts` pins it), and it is the
  pre-deploy half of V2-1.

#### Root cause [read]

- `guestResumeTarget` sends a guest's partial run opened on other parts to
  its own parts (`own`), and never asks whether those parts plan anything on
  the bike as it is now. `page.tsx` planned `?resume=own` as those parts; an
  empty plan is the empty page, which renders no wizard, so neither the
  wizard's own check nor its `?resume=full` was ever reached. Introduced with
  the `own` target in §5.12 (`fe50543` on the rebased branch, `b1ed4a4`
  before the rebase — [V2], read); the code the unit started from
  (`0ad0982`) never sent a run anywhere.
- A saved bike never had the loop: `savedResumeTarget` sends a run to the
  parts its rows tell only when the full checkup does NOT take it over, which
  needs an answer narrowed there (`carriedOnWhole` → `narrowedAnswer`), so
  never to parts whose plan is empty; and the full page always renders the
  wizard. Its empty page (`?parts=` on such parts) leads to the full
  checkup, which does.
- V2-2 and V2-3: sentences written for the cases the round had in mind. The
  bold rule's qualifier "where the run knows its parts", added in §5.12
  (`fe50543`), was dropped in §5.13 (`da8522b`; `git log -S`); the import
  paragraph described a run whose rear-caliper page holds it, which a touched
  KO is not.

#### Fix

- **`page.tsx` plans `?resume=own` on parts that ask nothing as
  `?resume=full` on them**: the full checkup for that run, with their empty
  plan as `widenedFrom`. The wizard's `guestResumeTarget` holds the run there
  (`carriedOnWhole` over an empty plan: no answer can be narrowed where
  nothing is asked) — or, if the stored run is another one, sends it to ITS
  own parts. So no address sends a guest's run to a page with no question.
- **The wizard says what that costs** (`showsNamedFirst`, `Wizard.tsx`): such
  a run's answers were given for parts the full checkup does not ask them
  about — the shifting answer given for the front derailleur now counts for
  the rear one and the right shifter — so, as for a saved bike's run carried
  on as the full checkup (§5.13, RA-3), the notice names the answered
  questions that name several parts and the wizard opens no later than the
  first of them. Also for a guest's run whose parts the catalogue no longer
  knows. A guest's run the full checkup takes over otherwise still opens where
  it was. (That last sentence went on "every answer there is asked about
  what it was given for", which the fourth verification showed untrue of a
  run whose own parts no longer ask ONE of its answers, V3-2: the rule now
  fires for it, and its "otherwise" is narrower — below.)
- **Not changed**: the empty page itself (RA-5, awaiting a ruling), and an
  address that names such parts WITHOUT `?resume=` — the bike page's
  "Reprendre le contrôle" for a guest, the parts panel — still lands on it:
  the server cannot see a guest's run, and the bike page does not plan. Its
  one link is now where the run is resumed. `savedResumeTarget`, already
  without the loop, and the planner.
- **Words** (V2-2, V2-3): CLAUDE.md's bold rule now says where an answer can
  count for more parts than it was given for — only on the full checkup,
  when that takes the run over, when nothing can tell its parts, or when
  they ask nothing — and that a saved bike's rows then move to their whole
  question's first part, verdict, note and symptoms kept; the backlog's
  "rewritten" sentence the same; the import paragraph (CLAUDE.md) and
  `lib/guest/import.ts`'s header say that "never acted on" is the IMPORT's,
  and that an unfinished import holding a touched KO or a withheld symptom
  is carried on as the full checkup by the account's first page — said,
  named, shown first — as the guest's own browser carries it on. The guest
  bullet, the scope rule's RA-5 sentence and the "nothing loops" sentence of
  CLAUDE.md, the headers of `page.tsx`, `scope.ts` and the wizard's
  functions, §5.13's sentence that "a guest's never needs" the cursor, and
  the backlog's RA-5 entry, which now says what the visitor sees.

#### What production rows, and browsers, may look like (nothing is rewritten)

No data migration, and nothing this fix reads was written differently. A
guest browser that holds an unfinished left-shifter run of the old planner,
or a run whose parts its rebuilt bike no longer asks, now resumes it on the
full checkup. The code of §5.12 and §5.13 is not on `origin/main`
(`git merge-base --is-ancestor`, read on 2026-10-23), so production never ran
the loop.

- `tests/e2e/checkup-resume.spec.ts`: [V2]'s V2-TRAP rewritten to assert, FR

#### The fourth verification: a guest's run whose own parts no longer ask ONE of its answers; a touched OK alone; an entry "not looked at" (V3, found 2026-10-23, fixed 2026-10-24)

**Who measured.** **[V3]** the fourth adversarial verification of
`w5/partial-checkup`, at `82266a5` (the tip pushed as PR #38), quoted from
its findings: jsdom with the real page, wizard and actions on PostgreSQL,
and Chromium on a production build with the test hooks. **[I7]** the
implementer of the same night, on the same branch from `82266a5`: [V3]'s
cases read in its file and its log, V3-PARTLY-EMPTY and V3-TOUCHED-ONLY
adopted into this tree, a probe over every pair of presets (an untracked
file, removed again), then the change, its tests and the measurements
below. All of [I7]'s figures are jsdom and PostgreSQL.

**Symptom.**

- **V3-2** [V3; the adopted case run by [I7] on `82266a5`'s code]: on
  `road-rim-2x11`, today's planner, the scope front derailleur + chain plans
  the chain's question and the shifting one, the latter of the front
  derailleur alone (`partIds` and `coveredParts` `[front-derailleur]`); both
  answered OK; the guest bike then a `gravel-1x11`, where that scope plans
  the chain's question alone. `?spec=` → `…&resume=own` → `…&resume=full`:
  "Contrôle complet", the notice naming « Essayer tous les rapports », now
  asked of the rear derailleur and the right shifter. `widenedFrom.steps`
  was `[chain-wear]`, so the cursor rule (`widenedFrom.steps.length === 0`)
  did not apply; the wizard opened on question 0, the shifting question
  being 22nd. Four sentences said that no other path counts an answer for
  more than it was given for: `scope.ts` ("the one case"), `Wizard.tsx`
  ("Otherwise never: the full checkup takes a guest's run over only when
  it asks every answer about what it was given for"), CLAUDE.md, and the
  Fix of this section above.
  [I7], the probe: every ordered pair of the seven presets, every one- and
  two-part pick of the catalogue, every question the pick plans on the
  first answered OK with today's planner, the bike then the second preset,
  kept when the pick still plans something there, lacks an answered
  question and the full checkup takes the run over (`carriedOnWhole`). Of
  those, 16 runs had the first named answer before the first open
  question, where the cursor matters: all `road-disc-2x12` then one of the
  four 1x presets, the front derailleur picked with the front or the rear
  caliper or pads. The front-caliper one, adopted: the pads' and the
  alignment's questions asked of the front caliper alone, the shifting one
  of the front derailleur; on `gravel-1x11` those parts ask the first two
  and not the third, so they do not hold the run, and the full checkup
  took it over — opening, on `82266a5`'s code, on the rotors' question
  (index 1), past the pads' answer (index 0), given for the front caliper
  and now counting for both. The same run on a saved bike, rebuilt the
  same way, opens on the pads' question (measured on this tree; a saved
  bike's rule — always — is unchanged since `82266a5`).
- **V3-1** [V3]: V3-TOUCHED-ONLY — a rear-caliper run of the planner of
  before 2026-10-22 on `gravel-1x11`, its one answer `hose-leak` OK,
  unfinished, imported: `PARTIAL`, `IN_PROGRESS`,
  `hose-leak=OK@brake-caliper-rear`. `?parts=brake-caliper-rear` holds it
  ("Contrôle partiel"); the banner page and `?parts=chain`: "Contrôle
  complet", the notice naming « Chercher une fuite de liquide »; the
  guest's own browser, the same run: `''` → `&resume=own` → `&resume=full`.
  V3-TOUCHED-ONLY-FINISH: finished from the banner, an earlier full
  checkup's two brake-line lines open: `FULL`, the row on the front lever,
  both lines `recheck-ok`, both levers and both calipers `OK`. CLAUDE.md's
  import paragraph said the account resumes such a run "on the parts its
  rows tell when those parts hold the run (a rear-caliper run's rows, a
  touched OK's included, name the rear caliper)"; `lib/guest/import.ts`,
  that an OK or a skip to a touched question "widens nothing, there or
  here". The same claim stood in CLAUDE.md's touched-answer sentence and
  in `scope.ts`'s header and `ScopeFacts.touched` ([I7], read).
- **V3-4** [V3]: `docs/backlog.md`'s guest-slot entry ended "What the new
  bike's pages then show of them was not looked at", which [V3] had
  measured: a guest's run left on a 2x carried on into the new 1x bike's
  full checkup, announced by the notice — in Chromium (the front
  derailleur's run, both projects, both languages) and in jsdom (the front
  derailleur and chain's).
- **V3-3 and RA-5** — not this unit's, by the maintainer: unchanged, and
  the backlog's RA-5 entry states both, for a ruling.

**Root cause** [read]. V3-2: `showsNamedFirst` keyed a guest's run on its
own parts' plan being EMPTY, the case this section was about. But
`carriedOnWhole` also takes a run over when its parts do not hold it, and
a guest's checkup belongs to the slot, not to the bike: once the guest
bike is replaced, an answer given on the old bike to a question narrowed
there can be one its parts no longer ask — and then every narrowed answer
of the run, not only that one, counts on the full checkup for each part its
question names. V3-1: the paragraph described a run with an answer narrowed
on its parts; with only a touched OK none is, and `carriedOnWhole`'s second
clause (no answer narrowed) hands it to the full checkup from every page but
its parts' own.

**Fix.**

- **`outgrowsParts(run, scope, scoped, facts)`** (`lib/checkup/scope.ts`):
  the page of the run's own parts does not hold it, or does not ask one of
  its answers the bike is still asked. `showsNamedFirst` asks it of
  `widenedFrom` for a guest's run (the case of parts the catalogue does not
  know, and a saved bike's "always", unchanged). It is true wherever the
  empty-plan test was, and also of a run of before 2026-10-22 answered
  beyond its parts (R1) or holding a touched answer or a withheld symptom,
  whose answers were given whole: nothing in a stored run says which bike or
  planner an answer was given under. So such a guest's run now opens no
  later than its first named answer too, as the same run imported onto an
  account already did. Its "otherwise" is now exact: the page of its parts
  holds the run, asks every answer the bike is still asked and narrows
  none, and the full checkup asks each exactly as that page does.
- **One test changed with it**: "an old guest state with a stale scope (R1)
  is read safely" (`tests/integration/checkup-resume.test.ts`) clicked OK on
  whatever the wizard opened on and expected one answer more. The wizard
  now opens on the pads' answer, already given: the case asserts that, then
  goes on to the first open question and answers it; every assertion it
  made is kept.
- **Words.** V3-2: `scope.ts` (the header, `carriedOnWhole`,
  `guestResumeTarget`'s last paragraph), `Wizard.tsx` (`showsNamedFirst`,
  and `resumeNoticeOf`'s "opens on the first" → "no later than"),
  `page.tsx`'s header ("when the full checkup asks every one of its answers
  about what it was given for" → "takes the run over", and "no later
  than"), the describe comment of `Wizard.test.tsx`, CLAUDE.md, and §5.13
  and this section above, each saying what it said. V3-1: CLAUDE.md's
  touched-answer sentence and its import paragraph, `lib/guest/import.ts`,
  and `scope.ts`'s header and `ScopeFacts.touched`. V3-4: the backlog entry,
  and the RA-5 entry names V3-3.

**What production rows, and browsers, may look like** (nothing is
rewritten). No data migration: the change moves where the wizard opens,
nothing it stores. The code of §5.12 onwards is not on `origin/main`.

**Tests** [I7].

- `lib/checkup/scope.test.ts`: `outgrowsParts` — a run its parts hold and
  ask whole (no), an answer the corpus dropped (decides nothing), parts that
  ask nothing, an answer its parts no longer ask, a run they do not hold, a
  touched OK, a withheld symptom (yes).
- `components/checkup/Wizard.test.tsx`: a guest's run whose own parts' page
  asks two of its three answers opens on the first named one, the notice
  naming it.
- `tests/integration/checkup-resume.test.ts`, a new block: V3-PARTLY-EMPTY
  adopted (the walk, the notice, the shifting question's parts, the answer
  not past, nothing written); its front-caliper variant, in the guest's
  browser and on a saved bike rebuilt the same way, both opening on the
  pads' answer; V3-TOUCHED-ONLY and V3-TOUCHED-ONLY-FINISH adopted, as the
  documents now describe them.

**Seen failing** [I7]: `Wizard.tsx` of `82266a5` put back, then restored and
compared with its saved copy (`Wizard.test.tsx`, and the §5.14 blocks of
`checkup-resume.test.ts`): the new case of `Wizard.test.tsx` and the
front-caliper variant (on the rotors' question) failed; the other three new
integration cases passed, as they describe what was already so. The R1
case, changed after that run, fails under the first mutation below, which

`scope.test.ts` and `Wizard.test.tsx`; int: the whole of

| Mutation                                                   | Failing       |
| ---------------------------------------------------------- | ------------- |

| `outgrowsParts` without "does not ask one of its answers"  | 1 fast        |

(A second case failed under the second mutation, "stays on a KO until a

## 6. How to detect a regression

**§2, advisories.**

- `npx audit-ci --config audit-ci.json` (CI's `audit` job) and
  `bash scripts/ci/trivy.sh`. On a day ids are landing, run `audit` more than
  once before believing a green (§2.6).
- `sprintf-js` coming back turns `audit` red: that is what the scoped override
  is for. `npm explain js-yaml@3.15.2` shows the override in force.
- Renovate's Dependency Dashboard (issue #11) is where a proposal against an
  `overrides` entry appears (§2.5).
- Both gates run on `main` once a day (§2.7, since 2026-10-19):
  `gh run list --workflow=advisories.yml --limit 5` shows the last runs, and
  an OPEN issue titled "Scheduled advisory check: main is red" is a red that
  nobody has fixed yet — the workflow comments on it and never closes it.
  `npx vitest run --project unit tests/unit/ci/required-checks.test.ts
tests/unit/ci/advisory-report.test.ts` holds the workflow's shape and the
  report script, with no network. Between two runs a green `main` on GitHub
  is still a statement about its last run: `bash scripts/ci/audit.sh` on a
  fresh `npm ci` of `main` is the check, and `npm view <pkg> time` beside the
  advisory's range says which release to take — not `npm audit`'s proposal.
  No run at all for days means the schedule itself stopped (GitHub disables
  one after 60 days without activity, per its documentation):
  `gh workflow enable advisories.yml`.

**§3, the environment contract.**

- `npx vitest run --project unit tests/unit/db tests/unit/deploy` — the
  contract's rules, `check-env`, `vercel-build.sh`'s refusals, the `[env]` line,
  the boot tier's link to the gate. No network, no database.
- `ENABLE_TEST_PAGES=1 NEXT_PUBLIC_TEST_HOOKS=1 bash scripts/ci/build.sh` — ends
  with the boot check and `npx --no-install vitest run --project boot`. Needs a
  reachable `_test` database, and tests the build it has just made (§3.7).
- `npx vitest run --project integration tests/integration/prisma-error-log.test.ts`
  — nothing about the limiter's expected miss on any console channel.
- On Vercel: the build log must carry
  `check-env: environment contract satisfied (VERCEL_ENV=<scope>, isProduction=<…>).`
  and the runtime log `[env] contract enforced (VERCEL_ENV=<scope>)`. A build
  that stops on `✗ VERCEL_ENV is not set` means the system-variables checkbox is
  off. `/api/health`: 200 is healthy, 503 is the database, a 401 on a preview is
  deployment protection, and **a 500 is the contract** (§3.2).

**§4, the two workflows.**

- `npx vitest run --project unit tests/unit/ci tests/unit/deploy` — the
  refusals, the success path, the summary, the workflow shapes, the validator's
  verdict. No network, no database.
- `npx vitest run --project integration tests/integration/migrate-preview.test.ts`
  — the real Prisma, green, on the tier's `_test` database.
- `bash scripts/ci/renovate-config.sh` — the real validator (seconds warm,
  ~350 MB cold).
- After a dispatch: `gh run view <run-id> --log`, and read `N migrations found`
  TOGETHER WITH Prisma's verdict under it. Then the two branches in the Neon
  console's SQL editor. `/api/health` cannot tell: it is a bare `SELECT 1`
  (`app/api/health/route.ts`), unchanged on purpose.

**§5, the launch checklist.**

- §5.1: `npx vitest run --project unit tests/unit/deploy/lib-nvm.test.ts`; and,
  on a machine with nvm, `npm run ci:local`, or `npm run lhci` after a build.
  Exit 127 with `npx: command not found` is this.
- §5.2: CI's `lighthouse` job on any pull request is the gate; locally,
  `bash scripts/ci/lighthouse.sh` after `bash scripts/ci/build.sh`, and note the
  machine's load beside the result.
- §5.3: `npx vitest run --project unit tests/unit/content/check.test.ts` on its
  own (33 tests, 8 s here). If it passes alone, the timeout measured the
  machine.
- §5.4: after an audit of production, the reports under `.lighthouseci/` must
  name the production host, not `localhost`, and no `next` process may have been
  started.
- §5.6, finding 1:
  `npx vitest run --project unit tests/unit/deploy/dev-script.test.ts`. In a
  dev server's log, `Module not found: Can't resolve '@/lib/content/generated/…'`
  is this, and `npm run content:generate` clears it without a restart.
- §5.7: `npm run e2e:docker -- --project=desktop-chromium --repeat-each=5
--retries=0 tests/e2e/guides-filter.spec.ts -g "a card opens its guide"`.
  Before a fix it fails some of the time in the emulated container; after one,
  never. A host run does not show it.
- §5.8: `npx vitest run --project ui components/bike` (the panel and the
  workspace; about 20 s), `npx vitest run --project integration
tests/integration/part-form.test.ts tests/integration/bikes.test.ts`, and —
  after the e2e build — `npx playwright test --project=desktop-chromium
--project=mobile-chromium tests/e2e/part-panel.spec.ts`. By hand, on a bike
  with a dropper post: "Fourche" in the list, the seatpost on the bike; the
  travel field must read 170, and clicking into it and out must leave no
  "Enregistré". And: a width typed, then "Pièces" pressed without leaving
  the field; the list must show it the next time the tyre is opened. And,
  since the review: `45.` typed in a width and "Pièces" pressed — the width
  must still be there the next time the tyre is opened, and a notice must
  have said the text was not saved; the same press with the network off
  must raise the notice too. And, since the second review, with the
  network throttled: a width typed, the browser's Back and then Forward
  without leaving the field, the width the page still shows typed again and
  the field left — its status must read "Enregistrement…", and the bike
  must end at that width, whether the first write was still on its way or
  had just answered (`lib/bike/own-writes.test.ts`, in the unit tier, is the
  record alone). And, since the third review, WITHOUT reloading anywhere on
  the way: a guest bike's width set and the field left; the header's link
  home, the demo, "Modifier ce vélo"; on the new bike the same width typed
  away and back, and the field left — no "Enregistré" may appear under
  it; and the same with the tree in place of the demo: its questions
  answered again, "Générer mon vélo", "Remplacer". And, since the fourth
  review — not by hand: a person's tab switch takes the focus out of the
  field first — `npx vitest run --project unit lib/bike/repo.test.ts` (a
  guest repo writes the bike it was built for, and no other) and, after the
  e2e build, `npx playwright test --project=desktop-chromium
tests/e2e/part-panel.spec.ts -g "another page of the same browser"`: two
  pages of one context, a width typed and not left on the first, the guest
  bike replaced on the second; the first must write nothing and say what
  was not saved. A new form that edits "the selected X" is tested
  through the container that outlives the selection, never alone — and its
  number fields are tried with text a number field cannot read.
- §5.9: `npx vitest run --project ui components/build-list tests/ui/guest-import.test.tsx`,
  `npx vitest run --project unit lib/shop lib/checkup lib/guest tests/unit/checkup`,
  `npx vitest run --project integration tests/integration/guest-import.test.ts tests/integration/build-lists.test.ts`,
  and — after the e2e build —
  `npx playwright test --project=desktop-chromium tests/e2e/build-list-long-answer.spec.ts`.
  By hand, as a guest: the demo bike, a checkup of the brakes with the pads
  worn, "Créer ma liste"; in "Référence des plaquettes" type or paste more
  than 64 characters. The field must stop at 64 and the list must still be
  there after a reload — "Rien à acheter pour l'instant" under a list that
  had lines is this. In a browser that may already be affected:
  `JSON.parse(localStorage["va:buildlist:demo"])` (or `…:local`) holding a
  `refinement` value longer than 64 characters while the list page shows its
  lines is the fix at work; the same value with an empty page is the defect.
  A new place that reads or bounds a refinement calls
  `lib/shop/refinement.ts`: one more copy of the number — there were six, and
  the note that counted them said five — is how this happened. The same for a
  key: `isRefinementKey`, never a pattern written out again. A row holding
  `constructor` among a refinement's keys that the list page still hands back
  to its action ("Modification non enregistrée" on every answer of one line,
  while a tick on it works) is the review's second finding;
  `npx vitest run --project security tests/security/guest-import.test.ts tests/security/build-list-actions.test.ts`
  holds the two ways into that column to one rule. A line holding more than
  24 answers whose next edit is refused `TOO_MANY` is V1-1 back:
  `npx vitest run --project integration tests/integration/build-lists.test.ts -t "ALREADY holds"`.
- §5.10: `npx vitest run --project integration tests/integration/checkup-resume.test.ts tests/integration/checkups.test.ts`,
  `npx vitest run --project security tests/security/checkup-input.test.ts`,
  `npx vitest run --project ui components/checkup/Wizard.test.tsx`, and —
  after the e2e build —
  `npx playwright test --project=desktop-chromium --project=mobile-chromium tests/e2e/checkup-resume.spec.ts tests/e2e/guest-import.spec.ts`.
  By hand, signed in, a saved bike: "Contrôler les pièces sélectionnées" on
  two parts — the chain and the front caliper — one answer, wait for
  "Sauvegardé", reload, one more answer, wait again — the run's `CheckupItem`
  rows must be two. The same through the bike's "Reprendre le contrôle":
  "Contrôle complet", the notice, both rows (the front caliper's row cannot
  tell front from both: §5.13).
  A save that answers ok while a run's row count falls is this defect; in the
  database, a `PARTIAL` run with no item that is NEW since the deploy is
  worth a look (the query is in §5.10). A tab left open on a bundle of
  before the deploy now gets "Impossible de sauvegarder ce contrôle…" on a
  resumed partial run, and its rows stay.
- §5.11: `npx vitest run --project unit lib/checkup tests/unit/checkup lib/guest/import.test.ts`
  (the one-sided cases of `line-identity` take about 4 s),
  `npx vitest run --project integration tests/integration/checkups.test.ts -t "limited to some parts"`,
  and the last two cases of `tests/e2e/checkup-resume.spec.ts`. By hand,
  signed in: a bike whose two calipers say "À surveiller", a checkup on the
  rear caliper alone, all OK — the front caliper must still say "À
  surveiller", the front lever nothing, and a front-pads line must stay
  open; the wizard must have said "ne comptera que pour : Plaquettes ou
  patins arrière". A `CheckupItem` of a `PARTIAL` checkup naming a part
  outside what was picked is this defect back — unless it is a row written

  in a caliper's run names the front lever).
- §5.12: the same commands as §5.10 and §5.11, and
  `npx vitest run --project unit lib/checkup/scope.test.ts --coverage` for
  the rule's 100 %. By hand, as a guest: the rear caliper, the pads "Ça ne
  marche pas" (worn), then the FRONT caliper picked — the address must come

  Signed in, since §5.13, the same: "Contrôle partiel", "Un contrôle limité
  à : Étrier de frein arrière…", the address `?parts=brake-caliper-rear`

- §5.13: the same commands, and
  `npx vitest run --project unit tests/unit/checkup/recorded-scope.test.ts`
  (the row reading replayed on every one- and two-part pick of the seven

- §5.14: `npx vitest run --project integration tests/integration/checkup-resume.test.ts -t "5.14"`
  (the walk: an address seen twice is the loop back), and — after the e2e

- §5.6, finding 2 (ruled on 2026-10-07, fixed by 2026-10-08):
  `npx vitest run --project unit lib/guest lib/checkup`,
  `npx vitest run --project ui tests/ui/guest-import.test.tsx`,
  `npx vitest run --project security tests/security/guest-import.test.ts`,
  `npx vitest run --project integration tests/integration/guest-import.test.ts`,
  and — after `ENABLE_TEST_PAGES=1 NEXT_PUBLIC_TEST_HOOKS=1 bash scripts/ci/build.sh` —
  `npx playwright test --project=desktop-chromium tests/e2e/guest-import.spec.ts`,
  which reads the imported rows from Postgres. No fixture writes `steps` any
  more: they all come from `tests/_helpers/guest-work.ts`. By hand, on
  production: a checkup made in the wizard as a guest, sign-up, `/import`,
  then look for a `Checkup` row on the imported bike.
